### [94] [Memory with Onsager-Casimir symmetry: Rotating particle in a viscoelastic fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00344)
*Debankur Das,Niloyendu Roy,Niklas Windbacher,Clemens Bechinger,Matthias Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了旋转布朗粒子在非马尔可夫流体中的随机动力学，发现旋转增强扩散并产生时间反对称交叉关联，通过引入最小线性模型，揭示了旋转导致的非互易记忆核及其满足的Onsager-Casimir对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索旋转布朗粒子在粘弹性（非马尔可夫）流体中的随机动力学行为，理解旋转如何影响粒子的扩散和涨落关系，特别是时间非局域随机动力学中Onsager-Casimir对称性的实验实现。

Method: 实验上，测量旋转布朗粒子在粘弹性流体中的长时间扩散和位移交叉关联。理论上，引入最小线性模型：示踪粒子通过平流耦合与慢浴变量耦合，消除浴变量后导出具有非互易记忆核的广义Langevin方程。利用Onsager-Casimir对称性和涨落-响应关系，构建几何构造将双时间交叉关联与粒子的横向响应联系起来。

Result: 实验发现旋转增强粒子的长时间扩散率，并在垂直于旋转轴的平面内产生正交位移分量之间的时间反对称交叉关联。理论得到的记忆核随时间旋转形成对数螺线，满足旋转矢量反演下的Onsager-Casimir对称性。导出的涨落-响应关系涉及响应的反对称部分，与普通Einstein关系不同。实验与理论定性地一致。

Conclusion: 旋转胶体在粘弹性流体中的动力学构成时间非局域随机动力学中Onsager-Casimir对称性的最小实现，旋转作为非平衡驱动力通过平流耦合产生非互易记忆效应，为理解非平衡统计力学中的对称性提供了简洁的实验平台。

Abstract: We study the stochastic dynamics of a rotating Brownian particle in a non-Markovian fluid. Experimentally, we find that rotation enhances the long-time diffusivity of the particle and generates time-antisymmetric cross-correlations between orthogonal displacement components in the plane perpendicular to the rotation axis. To rationalize these observations, we introduce a minimal linear model in which a tracer is coupled to a slow bath degree of freedom and rotation enters through an advective coupling. Eliminating the bath variable yields a generalized Langevin equation with a non-reciprocal memory kernel. This kernel rotates in time, forming a logarithmic spiral, and it obeys Onsager-Casimir symmetry under reversal of the rotation vector, and the corresponding fluctuation-response relation. From the latter we obtain a geometric construction that links two-time cross-correlations to the transverse response of the particle in bulk. Unlike the ordinary Einstein relation, this relation involves the antisymmetric sector of the response. Our experiments and theory are in qualitative agreement, establishing rotating colloids in viscoelastic fluids as a minimal realization of Onsager-Casimir symmetry in time-nonlocal stochastic dynamics

</details>


### [95] [Noise-enhanced temporal boundary states in non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01179)
*Jielong Zhang,Bozheng Xue,Xianfeng Man,Baizhan Xia*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了时间噪声对非厄米拓扑系统的影响，发现与直觉相反，时间噪声在系综平均下可指数增强时间界面处的拓扑边界态响应强度，并在机器人超材料网络中进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为噪声会削弱甚至破坏拓扑保护，是拓扑态的根本威胁。作者旨在挑战这一普遍认知，探索时间噪声在周期性驱动的非厄米拓扑系统中是否可能扮演建设性角色。

Method: 在二维周期驱动非厄米系统中引入两种时间噪声：周期性重复时间噪声和完全随机时间噪声。通过系综平均下的超算符（superoperator）分析，研究噪声对能带结构、增长模与衰减模的影响，并在机器人超材料网络中进行实验验证。

Result: 无噪声的能带结构在两种噪声下均得以保留；但噪声增强了增长模的增长率，同时抑制了衰减模的衰减率。系综平均下，时间界面处的响应强度呈指数增强。在机器人超材料网络中实验实现了噪声增强的时间边界态。

Conclusion: 时间噪声并非拓扑态的威胁，而可作为一种建设性因素，使拓扑态明确涌现，并赋予拓扑器件卓越的鲁棒性。这一发现颠覆了噪声有害的传统范式。

Abstract: Time-periodic modulation introduces a synthetic degree of freedom to manipulate topological phases. This synthetic dimension can trigger a phase transition that localizes a boundary state at the temporal interface. Noise is widely deemed a fundamental threat to topological protection, universally anticipated to weaken or even destroy topological states. Here, we introduce periodically repeated temporal noise and fully random temporal noise into a 2D periodically driven non-Hermitian system. Paradoxically, under ensemble averaging, such temporal noise drives an exponential enhancement of the response intensity at the temporal interface. An averaged superoperator analysis shows that the noiseless band structure is preserved under both types of noise. Yet the noise increases the growth rate of growing modes while suppressing the decay rate of decaying ones. Finally, we experimentally realize the noise-enhanced temporal boundary state in a robotic metamaterial network. This finding establishes temporal noise as a constructive ingredient, enabling the unambiguous emergence of topological states and conferring exceptional robustness upon topological devices.

</details>


### [96] [Algorithmic overlaps in the Baxter-Wu model: cluster dynamics under Novotny-Evertz updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01280)
*Ian Pilé,Lev Shchur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用 Novotny-Evertz 亚晶格冻结单簇更新算法，对 Baxter-Wu 模型进行马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟，研究连续自旋构型间的空间重叠（overlap）。结果表明，该算法重叠量的均值在临界区域表现为类序参量行为，在 Tc 处趋于零但不发散；其有限尺寸值呈幂律衰减，衰减指数 ψ=0.378(4)，不同于 Ising/Potts 模型在标准 FK 簇动力学下的 ~0.42，揭示该重叠反映的是算法动力学特性而非模型的静态普适性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三自旋相互作用的 Baxter-Wu 模型中，算法性可观测量（重叠量）在临界区的行为，检验其是否遵循热力学相变的标度规律，并厘清该重叠量反映的是模型静态普适类还是特定更新算法的动力学特征。

Method: 采用 Novotny-Evertz 亚晶格冻结单簇更新算法对 Baxter-Wu 模型进行大规模马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟；定义并追踪连续自旋构型间算法性重叠量的均值与方差，在跨越临界温度的区域进行有限尺寸标度分析，拟合重叠量在 Tc 处的幂律衰减形式 U₂(Tc) ~ L^(-ψ)。

Result: 重叠均值表现为类序参量：在有序相为有限平台值，穿越 Tc 后趋于零；在临界点处重叠量保持有限、不发散，其有限尺寸值服从干净的幂律衰减，跨越 11 个系统尺寸，得到衰减指数 ψ = 0.378(4)，显著小于 Ising 和 Potts 模型在标准 FK 簇动力学下的 ~0.42。

Conclusion: 算法性重叠量在 Baxter-Wu 模型中的临界行为反映了 Novotny-Evertz 亚晶格冻结动力学的特性，而非模型的静态普适类；该结论为区分“算法依赖”与“模型普适”的可观测量提供了清晰证据。

Abstract: We study the spatial overlap of successive spin configurations generated by Markov chain Monte Carlo simulations of the Baxter-Wu model. Using the Novotny-Evertz sublattice-freezing single-cluster update, we track the mean and variance of the algorithmic overlap across the critical region. We show that, even in this three-spin model, the overlap acts as an algorithmic observable that follows the thermodynamics of the transition: the single-cluster overlap mean behaves like an order parameter, dropping from a finite ordered-phase plateau toward zero across $T_c$. The overlap does not diverge at criticality, instead it remains finite and its finite-size value decays as a clean power law, $U_2(T_c)\sim L^{-ψ}$, over eleven sizes with an exponent $ψ{=}0.378(4)$ smaller than the value $\approx0.42$ found for the Ising and Potts models under standard Fortuin-Kasteleyn cluster dynamics, indicating that it reflects the Novotny-Evertz sublattice-freezing dynamics rather than any static property of the model.

</details>


### [97] [LieStoNet: Learning Lie Symmetries from Spatiotemporal Data for Stochastic Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01582)
*Shida Liu,Abhishek Gupta,Sumit Sinha,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: LieStoNet 是一个无需模板的端到端框架，直接从时空轨迹中发现随机微分方程（SDE）的李点对称性，无需预设对称群或正则坐标。该框架通过学习漂移和扩散的神经代理，并强制满足SDE确定方程与李代数公理来发现可投影生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性在现代机器学习和物理学中至关重要，能提升样本效率、鲁棒性和分布外泛化能力。然而，随机动力系统的连续对称性通常未知，SDE的对称性发现领域几乎未被探索。现有方法依赖预定义模板或正则坐标，缺乏从数据中自动发现SDE对称性的通用框架。

Method: 基于Gaeta和Quintero (1999)的SDE李对称理论，LieStoNet框架包含：(1) 从增量数据中学习漂移项和扩散项的神经代理模型；(2) 通过强制满足SDE确定方程来学习可投影生成元；(3) 分别正则化以确保李括号闭合性、遵守李代数公理（双线性、反对称性、雅可比恒等式）以及非冗余独立基；(4) 代理模型同时定义关联的Fokker-Planck方程，可选并行发现其李点对称性。

Result: 在多个具有已知解析对称性的典型SDE上，LieStoNet恢复的生成元与真实对称代数一致，为含噪动力学提供了可解释的对称性发现。

Conclusion: LieStoNet 首次实现了从数据中对SDE李点对称性的自动无模板发现，填补了该领域空白，并展示了其在含噪动力系统中进行可解释对称性发现的潜力。

Abstract: Symmetry is central to modern machine learning and physics: invariances and equivariances improve sample efficiency, robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization, while symmetry principles guide scientific modeling. Yet for stochastic dynamical systems the relevant continuous symmetries are rarely known, and symmetry discovery for SDEs has remained essentially unexplored. We introduce \textit{LieStoNet}, an end-to-end, \emph{template-free} framework for discovering Lie-point symmetries of SDEs directly from spatiotemporal trajectories, without prespecifying symmetry groups, templates, or canonical coordinates. Building on the seminal SDE Lie-symmetry theory of Gaeta and Quintero (1999), which formalizes Lie-point SDE symmetries and their relation to Fokker-Planck symmetries, LieStoNet learns neural surrogates for drift and diffusion from increments, then learns projectable generators by enforcing the SDE determining equations, separately regularizing for closure under Lie brackets, adherence to the Lie algebra axioms (bilinearity, antisymmetry, Jacobi), and a non-redundant independent basis. The surrogate also defines an associated Fokker-Planck equation, enabling optional discovery of its Lie-point symmetries in parallel. Across multiple canonical SDEs with known analytic symmetries, LieStoNet recovers generators consistent with the ground-truth symmetry algebra, providing interpretable symmetry discovery for noisy dynamics. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/sumit-sinha-seas/LieStoNet_Final.git}{this link}.

</details>


### [98] [Universal Scaling of the Magnetocaloric Effect in 2D Ising Monolayers and Bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01811)
*Basit Iqbal,Kingshuk Sarkar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究二维铁磁Ising模型在六种晶格结构中的磁热效应，发现磁熵变和场指数遵循与配位数和层数无关的普适标度行为，为磁性制冷剂设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究二维铁磁体系中晶格几何结构（配位数）和层数对磁热效应的影响，并验证磁热效应是否存在普适标度行为。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟，结合Binder累积量确定临界温度；计算磁熵变、绝热温变、磁Grüneisen参数、相对冷却功率和冷却能力；通过归一化和温度标度分析验证普适性；进行临界标度和磁滞分析。

Result: 临界温度随配位数增加而升高；磁熵变峰值随配位数增加而降低，但磁熵变和场指数经归一化后坍缩为普适主曲线；绝热温变趋势相反；相对冷却功率和冷却能力近似不变；低配位数晶格的磁滞回线宽度随温度下降更快。

Conclusion: 二维单层和双层磁性晶格的磁热效应展现出与配位数和层数无关的普适标度行为，可用于指导磁性制冷材料的设计。

Abstract: We report a Monte Carlo study of the magnetocaloric effect (MCE) in two-dimensional ferromagnetic Ising models on square, honeycomb, and triangular lattices with monolayer and bilayer configurations. Using Binder cumulant analysis, we determine the critical temperature ($T_c$) of each structure and find that $T_c$ increases with coordination number, from the honeycomb ($r=3$) to the triangular ($r=6$) lattice. In contrast, the magnetic entropy change ($-ΔS_M$) decreases with coordination number, reaching its maximum for the honeycomb lattice. After normalization by their peak values and appropriate temperature scaling, both $-ΔS_M$ and the field exponent $n$ collapse onto universal master curves for different magnetic fields and across all six lattice structures at a fixed low field. This demonstrates universal MCE scaling independent of coordination number and layer count. Critical scaling analysis further supports the observed universality and power-law behavior. Unlike $-ΔS_M$, the adiabatic temperature change ($ΔT_{ad}$) increases with coordination number, whereas the magnetic Grüneisen parameter ($Γ_M$) follows the same trend as $-ΔS_M$. Although the peak value of $-ΔS_M$ decreases with coordination number, the relative cooling power and cooling capacity remain nearly unchanged due to compensating broadening of the $-ΔS_M$ curves. The field dependence of $-ΔS_M$, relative cooling power, and cooling capacity follows power laws up to $\sim2.6$ T (assuming $J\approx1$ meV). Hysteresis analysis shows that lattices with lower coordination numbers exhibit a faster reduction in loop width with increasing temperature. These results establish the universal scaling behavior of the magnetocaloric effect in two-dimensional monolayer and bilayer magnetic lattices and provide guidelines for designing magnetic refrigerants.

</details>


### [99] [Gap distributions between successive personal bests in cricket: Data and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01882)
*Priyanka D. Bhoyar,Prashant M. Gade*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了板球运动员职业生涯中个人最佳成绩间的间隔分布，发现其遵循截断幂律而非经典记录理论预测的1/g分布，揭示了职业生涯演化对记录统计的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典记录理论预测独立同分布序列的记录间隔服从普适的1/g分布，但运动员职业生涯受学习、衰老、能力变化等非平稳因素影响。作者旨在检验这一理论预测在真实体育数据中是否成立，并探究职业生涯的时间结构如何塑造个人最佳成绩的统计规律。

Method: 利用ESPN Cricinfo获取的顶级Test、ODI和T20板球运动员职业生涯数据，计算连续个人最佳成绩之间的比赛局数间隔g；用截断幂律P(g)∝g^{-α}e^{-λg}拟合经验分布；通过Bootstrap重排（保持个人得分分布和生涯长度但破坏时间顺序）构建零模型，比较原始数据与重排数据的指数α差异。

Result: 经验分布服从截断幂律，指数α在0.799至0.843之间，显著偏离i.i.d.预测的α=1。破坏时间顺序后，α升至0.939–0.979，表明职业演化是造成偏离的关键因素。个人最佳成绩的进展保留了球员职业生涯时间组织的信息，无法用简单随机记录过程完全解释。

Conclusion: 记录统计在非平稳、路径依赖系统中会发生显著改变；个人最佳成绩序列不仅是随机过程的产物，更是运动员职业发展轨迹的统计印记。这一发现对理解更广泛的非平稳系统中的极值统计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Successive personal best performances provide a natural measure of progression in an athlete's career. Classical record theory predicts a universal gap distribution, $P(g)\sim 1/g$, for independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) sequences. However, sporting careers are shaped by learning, aging, changes in ability, and external influences that violate these assumptions. We investigate the statistics of inter-record gaps, defined as the number of innings between successive personal best scores, in cricket. Using career records of leading Test, ODI, and T20 players obtained from ESPN Cricinfo. We find that the empirical distributions are well described by truncated power law $P(g) \propto g^{-α} e^{-λg}$ with exponents in the range (0.799 $\leq α\leq$ 0.843). Much of this deviation disappears when the temporal ordering of innings is destroyed, indicating that career evolution plays a key role in shaping record occurrence. Bootstrap-shuffled careers, which preserve individual score distributions and career lengths while removing temporal ordering, yield significantly larger exponents ($α\approx 0.939\text{--}0.979$). These findings show that the progression of personal best performances retains information about the temporal organization of a player's career and cannot be fully explained by simple stochastic record processes. More generally, they illustrate how record statistics are altered in nonstationary and path-dependent systems.

</details>


### [100] [Inhomogeneous Ising Model on 2D kagomé Lattice: Fermionic field approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02156)
*Shahane A. Khachatryan,Zhidong Zhang,Ara G. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过将二维非均匀伊辛模型映射到非对称八顶点模型并利用费米子表示，推导了kagomé晶格上该模型的配分函数、热力学量及临界曲面的精确表达式，并在极限情况下重现了方晶格和一维伊辛模型的已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维非均匀伊辛模型在kagomé晶格上的精确解尚未被系统研究，该模型在凝聚态物理（尤其是几何阻挫磁性系统）中具有重要理论意义，且需要验证该方法在已知极限下能否正确还原经典结果。

Method: 将kagomé晶格上的二维非均匀伊辛模型映射到特定的非对称八顶点模型，构造相应的R-矩阵，并采用费米子表示方法计算配分函数，进而在热力学极限下导出临界曲面方程及热力学量表达式。

Result: 获得了自由能、比热和铁磁情况下的自发磁化强度的显式表达式；推导了决定相变的临界曲面的精确方程；验证了当一或两个耦合常数趋于零时，模型分别退化为方晶格和一维伊辛模型，且临界耦合和自由能均与已知精确结果一致。

Conclusion: 该工作成功获得了kagomé晶格上二维非均匀伊辛模型的精确解，临界曲面方程和热力学量均以闭合形式给出，且该框架在退化极限下自洽地还原了经典伊辛模型结果，验证了方法的正确性与普适性。

Abstract: We investigate the two-dimensional inhomogeneous Ising model (2DIM) on the kagom'e lattice by mapping it onto a particular non-symmetric eight-vertex model and constructing the corresponding $R$-matrix. Using a fermionic representation, we evaluate the partition function and derive explicit expressions for the main thermodynamic quantities. In the thermodynamic limit, we obtain an exact equation for the critical surface determining the phase transition of the model. We also calculate the free energy, specific heat, and spontaneous magnetization in the ferromagnetic case. Furthermore, we show that when one or two coupling constants vanish, the model reduces, respectively, to the square-lattice and one-dimensional Ising models. In both limits, our results reproduce the corresponding exact critical couplings and free energies.

</details>


### [101] [Front Selection Is Not Determined by Renormalization-Group Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02211)
*Ko Okumura*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用重整化群框架证明FKPP方程的行波解构成连续RG不动点族，所有v≥2的前沿均为RG稳定，但渐近选择的前沿不由RG稳定性决定，揭示RG不动点稳定性与渐近态选择是不同概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FKPP方程是前沿传播和渐近态选择的范例，但重整化群（RG）框架中不动点稳定性与渐近态选择之间的关系尚不明确。作者旨在通过FKPP方程这一具体例子，厘清RG稳定性与渐近态选择是否为同一概念。

Method: 采用统一的重整化群（RG）框架对FKPP方程的行波解进行分析。将行波解视为RG不动点，考察其连续族的构成，并对不同速度（v≥2）的前沿解进行RG稳定性分析。

Result: 行波解形成连续的RG不动点族；所有速度v≥2的前沿解均为RG稳定；然而，渐近选择的前沿（即实际物理系统中被选中的解）并非由RG稳定性判定，两者出现了分离。

Conclusion: FKPP方程提供了一个显式反例，证明在RG框架中，不动点的稳定性与渐近态选择是两个彼此独立的概念，打破了将两者等同的传统直觉。

Abstract: The Fisher--Kolmogorov--Petrovsky--Piskunov equation provides a paradigmatic example of front propagation and asymptotic-state selection. Using a unified renormalization-group (RG) framework, we show that its traveling-wave solutions form a continuous family of RG fixed points and that all fronts with $v \ge 2$ are RG-stable. Nevertheless, the asymptotically selected front is not determined by RG stability. The FKPP equation therefore provides an explicit example in which RG fixed-point stability and asymptotic-state selection are distinct concepts.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum resetting with memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02297)
*Gabriele de Mauro,Manas Kulkarni,Satya N. Majumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种具有均匀记忆的量子随机重置协议，系统以均匀概率重置到历史中任意时刻访问过的状态。在能量本征基下，推导出任意不含时哈密顿量下密度矩阵元的精确演化，发现动力学仅通过玻尔频率依赖于哈密顿量。据此自然区分出有隙系统（离散谱）和无隙系统（连续谱）：前者非对角元呈现对数时间振荡的代数衰减，趋于与重置率无关的稳态；后者无法达到稳态，位置分布以普适的超慢尺度 log(rt)/r 扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典优先重定位模型推广到量子领域，探索量子系统在具有均匀历史记忆的重置协议下的非幺正、非马尔可夫动力学行为。

Method: 在能量本征基下，对任意不含时哈密顿量，解析推导密度矩阵所有矩阵元的精确时间演化方程，揭示动力学仅通过玻尔频率依赖于哈密顿量，并据此区分有隙与无隙量子系统分别分析。

Result: 有隙系统中，对角元不变，非对角元以连续变化的指数代数衰减，振幅在 log t 上周期振荡，趋于与重置率无关且保留初态记忆的稳态；无隙系统中，任意小的玻尔频率阻止稳态形成，位置分布以普适尺度 log(rt)/r 超慢扩散，与初态及哈密顿量细节无关。

Conclusion: 该量子重置协议揭示了有隙与无隙量子系统本质不同的长时行为，并展示了量子系统与其经典对应物之间的显著差异。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum stochastic resetting protocol with uniform memory, in which each resetting event returns the system to a state visited at a time chosen uniformly from its entire history. The resulting dynamics is nonunitary, non-Markovian and a direct quantum generalization of the classical preferential relocation model. Working in the energy eigenbasis, we derive the exact evolution of every density-matrix element for an arbitrary time-independent Hamiltonian and show that the Hamiltonian enters the dynamics only through the corresponding Bohr frequencies. This leads to a natural distinction between two classes of quantum systems: gapped and gapless. In \emph{gapped systems} (systems with a discrete energy spectrum), while the diagonal elements remain unchanged, the off-diagonal elements of the density matrix in the energy eigenbasis decay algebraically with a continuously varying exponent and with an amplitude that oscillates periodically in $\log t$. The system therefore approaches a stationary state that is independent of the resetting rate and retains a strong memory of the initial state. In \emph{gapless systems} (systems with a continuous energy spectrum), arbitrarily small Bohr frequencies prevent stationarity. Instead, the position distribution spreads on the universal (ultra-slow) scale $\log(rt)/r$, independently of the initial state and of the details of the Hamiltonian. We illustrate these results with a two-level system, a harmonic oscillator, and a free quantum particle, and contrast them with their classical counterparts.

</details>

### [103] [Rhombohedral Graphene: A Tale of Many Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00167)
*Ahmed Abouelkomsan,Filippo Gaggioli,Daniele Guerci,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于非相互作用能带色散的简单指标，用于识别菱方石墨烯相图中强关联区域。结合神经网络变分蒙特卡洛与Hartree-Fock理论，揭示了多种无经典对应物的电子晶体，包括Wigner晶体、自掺杂Wigner晶体和"反晶体"（电子液体中的空穴晶格）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱方石墨烯实验展现出丰富的关联量子相（手性超导体、电子晶体等），但缺乏系统的理论框架来预测和识别强关联区域，以及理解其中涌现的非常规电子晶态。

Method: 1. 从非相互作用能带色散中导出一个简单指标，用于定位强关联区域；2. 开发神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（NN-VMC）方法，结合Hartree-Fock理论，求解相互作用基态。

Result: 1. 指标可有效识别菱方石墨烯中随载流子浓度和位移场变化的强关联区域；2. 揭示了多种无经典对应物的电子晶体：随密度增加依次出现Wigner晶体、自掺杂Wigner晶体以及"反晶体"（电子液体中的空穴晶格）。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解菱方石墨烯中的关联量子相提供了简洁的理论判据和强大的数值工具，预言的新型电子晶体有望在实验中被观测，并可能与超导电性存在关联。

Abstract: Experiments on rhombohedral graphene have uncovered an extraordinary wealth of correlated quantum phases - from chiral superconductors to electronic crystals - all within a single family of atomically thin materials. Here, we introduce a simple indicator, derived from the noninteracting band dispersion, that identifies strongly correlated regions in the phase diagram of rhombohedral graphene as a function of carrier density and displacement field. We develop a neural-network variational Monte Carlo method, combined with Hartree-Fock theory, to solve the interacting ground states. Our calculation reveals a variety of electron crystals with no classical analog. These include, at increasing density: Wigner crystal, self-doped Wiger crystal, as well as ''anticrystal'', a lattice of holes in an electron liquid. We discuss their experimental manifestations and possible connection to superconductivity.

</details>


### [104] [Shape of Wigner Crystals and Hole Self-Doping in a Mexican-Hat Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00170)
*Minho Luke Kim,Xiao-Gang Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用变分蒙特卡罗方法研究墨西哥帽状色散关系下由强库仑相互作用诱导的维格纳晶体，发现优化轨道形状（使k=0附近电子耗尽）可降低基态能量，并揭示了空穴自掺杂效应在维格纳晶体相变附近的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上观察到随着电子密度降低，体系经历两次相变：从圆盘状费米面的自旋-谷极化费米液体，到环状费米面的费米液体，最终形成维格纳晶体。本文旨在从理论上重现这一相变序列，并理解环状费米面几何下维格纳晶体的形成机制及能量优化策略。

Method: 采用变分蒙特卡罗（VMC）方法。构建包含Jastrow因子和电子-空穴关联因子的多体波函数，通过优化轨道形状（使k=0附近电子密度耗尽）来最小化维格纳晶体的基态能量，并标定有效能带参数c₂和c₄以匹配实验观测的两步相变。

Result: （1）低基态能量要求在k=0附近耗尽电子的轨道形状；（2）即使采用该优化轨道，在维格纳晶体相变附近，若计入电子-空穴关联，k=0附近具有空穴自掺杂的维格纳晶体在能量上仍然更有利；（3）给出了掺杂空穴的色散关系估计。

Conclusion: 在墨西哥帽色散的环状费米面体系中，电子-空穴关联效应对于准确描述维格纳晶体相变至关重要：空穴自掺杂维格纳晶体在相变附近可能是真正的基态，这为理解低密度二维电子系统中的量子相变提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We study Wigner crystals (WCs) induced by a strong Coulomb interaction from the ring-like Fermi surface of a Mexican-hat dispersion $ε_k= c_2k^2+c_4k^4$. We design orbital shape in order to minimize the energy of the WC, and find that a low ground-state energy requires an orbital shape with a depletion of electrons near $k=0$. To capture the Coulomb-induced correlations, we include a Jastrow factor as well as a factor describing the correlation between electrons and doped vacancies. Using variational Monte Carlo calculations, we calibrate the effective band parameters $c_2$ and $c_4$ to reproduce the two transitions observed experimentally as the electron density is lowered: from a spin-valley-polarized Fermi liquid with a disk-like Fermi surface, to one with a ring-like Fermi surface, and finally to a WC. We find that, even with an optimized orbital shape that depletes electrons near $k=0$, a WC with hole self-doping near $k=0$ can still be energetically favorable near the WC transition, provided that the electron-vacancy correlation is included. We also estimate the dispersion of the doped hole.

</details>


### [105] [Chiral Magnon Mixing by Symmetry-Breaking in Collinear Ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00210)
*Dhurba R. Jaishi,Tyler J. Slade,S. X. M. Riberolles,Bing Li,Tianxiong Han,D. M. Pajerowski,D. L. Abernathy,Barry Winn,Melissa Graves-Brook,B. G. Ueland,R. J. McQueeney*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: RMn₆Sn₆ (R = Tb, Er) 铁磁体中存在左右手性磁振子带，它们在有限动量处交叉，其简并性可通过稀土磁各向异性或外场调控，形成节点线或带隙，带隙模式中包含k依赖的杂化手性激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，铁磁体中仅存在右手性磁振子，反铁磁体中左右手性磁振子在无外场时简并。铁磁体具有未补偿磁亚晶格，理论上应同时存在左右手性磁振子且能量被内部分子场劈裂，但其在动量空间中的色散关系及手性混合行为尚未被充分研究。

Method: 以RMn₆Sn₆ (R = Tb, Er) 铁磁体为模型体系，结合对称性分析，研究左右手性磁振子带在动量空间中的交叉行为。通过调控稀土磁各向异性或施加外场改变铁磁序的对称性，分析带交叉演变为节点线或被打开带隙的条件，并表征带隙模式中杂化手性激发的k依赖性。

Result: 在RMn₆Sn₆铁磁体的基面内，左右手性磁振子带在有限动量k处发生交叉，定义了具有相反动力学手性的模式。通过改变稀土磁各向异性或施加外场调节铁磁序对称性，该带交叉可保持为节点线，也可被打开带隙。带隙模式中包含杂化的手性激发，其手性特征依赖于动量k。

Conclusion: 铁磁体为研究左右手性磁振子的相互作用及杂化提供了天然平台，其手性带交叉的拓扑特性（节点线或带隙）可通过磁各向异性和外场灵活调控，这对理解磁性材料中的手性自旋动力学及潜在的自旋电子学应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Magnons in ferromagnets possess spin angular momentum defined by right-handed precession of the moment around the magnetization direction. In antiferromagnets with no net magnetization, left-and right-handed magnons are degenerate in the absence of an applied field. Ferrimagnets possess uncompensated magnetic sublattices, which should natively possess right-and left-handed magnons where their energy is split by the internal molecular field. Here, we show that RMn6Sn6 (R = Tb, Er) ferrimagnets possess right and left-handed magnon bands that cross at finite momentum (k) within the basal plane, defining modes with opposite dynamical chirality. Depending on the symmetry of the ferrimagnetic order, which may be manipulated by varying the rare-earth magnetic anisotropy or with applied field, the band crossing may remain a nodal line or may be gapped. The gapped modes contain hybridized chiral excitations whose chirality becomes k-dependent.

</details>


### [106] [Interplay of spin-orbit coupling, crystal field splitting and correlations: a ghost rotationally invariant slave boson treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00254)
*Xue Sun,Walber Hugo Brito,Andreas Gleis,Ran Adler,Tsung-Han Lee,Gabriel Kotliar,Corey Peters*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用鬼旋转不变奴隶玻色子方法（GRISB）研究t2g Hubbard-Kanamori模型中自旋轨道耦合、晶体场劈裂与电子关联的相互作用，以Sr₂RuO₄为案例，考察了GRISB向DMFT的收敛性，并揭示了Hubbard U增强SOC而Hund耦合J抑制SOC的规律，同时研究了费米面形状及应变驱动的Lifshitz相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋轨道耦合（SOC）、晶体场劈裂和电子关联在强关联电子材料中的相互作用是多体物理的核心问题。Sr₂RuO₄作为典型的关联多轨道体系，其低能物理由t2g轨道主导。传统DMFT方法在低温和宽参数范围下的计算代价高昂，而新近提出的GRISB方法有望以较低计算成本逼近DMFT精度，但该方法的收敛性及其对SOC相关物理量的描述能力尚需系统检验。

Method: 作者采用鬼旋转不变奴隶玻色子方法（GRISB）求解t2g Hubbard-Kanamori模型。模型参数由密度泛函理论（DFT）和线性化准粒子自洽GW（LQSGW）计算提取。通过逐步增加鬼（ghost）的数量，系统检验GRISB在静态和动力学观测量上向DMFT及实验结果的收敛行为。研究覆盖了较宽的Hubbard U和Hund耦合J参数范围，并考察了SOC驱动的静态与动力学响应。此外，还比较了不同方法学下的费米面形状，分析了应变驱动的Lifshitz相变。

Result: 1. GRISB对大多数可观测量收敛迅速，验证了该方法在描述SOC相关物理时的有效性和准确性。2. Hubbard U增强自旋轨道耦合效应，而Hund耦合J则抑制SOC。3. 不同方法学计算得到的费米面形状存在差异，并通过应变调控观察到了Lifshitz相变。

Conclusion: GRISB方法能够以较高的计算效率逼近DMFT精度，是研究关联多轨道体系中SOC效应的有力工具。U和J对SOC的相反调控效应揭示了关联效应与自旋轨道物理之间复杂的竞争机制，对理解Sr₂RuO₄等关联材料的电子结构具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay of spin-orbit coupling, crystal field splittings, and electronic correlations in the $t_{2g}$ Hubbard-Kanamori model within the recently formulated ghost rotationally invariant slave-boson method (GRISB). In particular, we study a tight binding model of Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ with parameters extracted from density functional theory and linearized quasiparticle self-consistent GW (LQSGW) calculations; we study the behavior of different physical quantities as the number of ghosts increases to examine the convergence of GRISB to dynamical mean field theory (DMFT) and experimental results; and we leverage the ability of GRISB to investigate the model over a wide range of parameters at low temperature. In particular, we examine both static and dynamical observables driven by the spin orbit coupling (SOC) and study how they vary as a function of the Hubbard $U$ and Hund's coupling $J$. GRISB converges quickly for most of these observables, and the calculations reveal the following: $U$ enhances the spin-orbit coupling while $J$ suppresses it. We also study the shape of the Fermi surface within different methodologies, and examine the Lifshitz transition which takes place as a function of strain in this material.

</details>


### [107] [Breakdown of Universal Whirling Order in a Heisenberg Tsai-Type Approximant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00467)
*Farid Labib,Guillaume Beutier,Alessandro Bombardi,Marc de Boissieu,Ryuji Tamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过X射线共振磁散射测量，对比Heisenberg（Au-Al-Gd）与非Heisenberg（Au-Al-Tb）Tsai型准晶近似物，发现非共面涡旋磁序的"普适性"在Heisenberg极限下被打破——前者展现出与涡旋序模型不兼容的方位角依赖，表明该磁序并非仅由Tsai团簇几何决定，而是需要自旋各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验近期提出的"非共面涡旋磁序是Tsai型准晶近似物中普适磁态"这一假说，探究其在Heisenberg极限下是否依然成立。

Method: 利用X射线共振磁散射（XRMS）技术，测量Heisenberg体系Au-Al-Gd与非Heisenberg体系Au-Al-Tb 1/1近似物的方位角依赖关系，对比两者的磁结构特征。

Result: 非Heisenberg的Au-Al-Tb符合已知的涡旋磁结构模型；而Heisenberg的Au-Al-Gd展现出定性不同的方位角依赖，与涡旋序模型不兼容。两者虽具有近乎相同的晶体结构和相当的磁能量尺度，却稳定于不同的反铁磁基态（传播矢量均为k=(1,0,0)），表明Heisenberg极限下存在一个近简并磁态的竞争流形。

Conclusion: 涡旋磁序的普适性并非Tsai团簇几何的必然结果，而是依赖自旋各向异性的存在；在Heisenberg极限下，多种近简并磁态相互竞争，打破了涡旋序的普适图像。

Abstract: Noncoplanar whirling magnetic order has recently been proposed as a universal magnetic state in non-Heisenberg Tsai-type quasicrystal approximants. Here, X-ray resonant magnetic scattering measurements on Heisenberg and non-Heisenberg Au-Al-R (R = Gd, Tb) 1/1 approximants reveal a clear breakdown of this universality in the Heisenberg limit. While the non-Heisenberg Au-Al-Tb system is well described by the previously identified whirling magnetic structure, the Heisenberg Au-Al-Gd counterpart exhibits qualitatively different azimuthal-angle dependences that are incompatible with the universal whirling-order model. Despite nearly identical crystal structures and comparable magnetic energy scales, the two systems stabilize distinct antiferromagnetic ground states with the same propagation vector k = (1, 0, 0), demonstrating that the apparent universality of whirling order is not a consequence of Tsai-cluster geometry alone but requires spin anisotropy. These results further reveal the emergence of a competing manifold of nearly degenerate magnetic states in the Heisenberg limit.

</details>


### [108] [Negative thermal expansion, lattice dynamics, and complex magnetism in TbFeO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00469)
*Shubham Farswan,Reshma Kumawat,Dipankar Sarkar,Deeksha Singh,Md. Atif Hasan,Devajyoti Mukherjee,Kaushik Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过X射线衍射、磁性测量和拉曼散射等手段，系统研究了TbFeO₃在5-300 K温区的晶格动力学、自旋-声子耦合及双磁振子激发，揭示了负热膨胀、声子频率反常及新兴磁性-晶格异常之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究TbFeO₃中晶格动力学与磁性自由度之间的耦合机制，特别是自旋-声子耦合及局域磁性-晶格异常的起源，以深入理解该体系中的多物理场相互作用。

Method: 采用变温X射线衍射（5-300 K）、直流磁化率测量、变温拉曼散射以及室温X射线光电子能谱（XPS）对TbFeO₃进行系统表征。通过分析晶格参数随温度的变化、拉曼声子频率及线宽偏离经典Klemens非谐衰减模型的行为、以及双磁振子激发的温度演化，揭示自旋-晶格耦合特征。

Result: XRD显示5-300 K范围内存在负热膨胀，降温时晶胞体积小幅单调增加，且无结构相变。拉曼声子频率偏离Klemens模型，表明存在自旋-声子耦合；A_g和B_1g对称性模式从低温高斯线型过渡到高温高斯-洛伦兹混合线型。高能区双磁振子激发与线性自旋波理论一致，其谱权重温度依赖性弱。约175 K以下出现一宽拉曼模式，呈类序参量演化，并与声子反常起始温度重合，而双磁振子响应未见相应强异常。

Conclusion: TbFeO₃是一个晶格动力学、自旋关联与新兴局域磁性-晶格异常之间存在显著相互作用的体系。约175 K处出现的宽拉曼模式及其与声子反常的同步性，暗示可能存在一种尚未被完全认识的局域磁性-晶格耦合机制。

Abstract: We report a temperature-dependent investigation of orthoferrite TbFeO$_3$ using x-ray diffraction, DC magnetization, and Raman scattering, complemented by room-temperature x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy. X-ray diffraction reveals negative thermal expansion over 5-300 K, with a small but systematic increase in unit-cell volume upon cooling in the absence of any structural phase transition. Raman scattering measurements identify the Raman-active phonon modes and show clear deviations from the conventional Klemens anharmonic decay model, particularly in phonon frequencies, indicating the presence of spin-phonon coupling. Two modes of $A_g$ and $B_{1g}$ symmetry exhibit a crossover from Gaussian-dominated line shapes at low temperatures to mixed Gaussian-Lorentzian profiles at higher temperatures, reflecting a transition from inhomogeneous broadening to lifetime-driven dynamics. High-energy Raman spectra reveal two-magnon excitations associated with the Fe sublattice, consistent with linear spin-wave theory, whose spectral weight shows only weak temperature dependence. In addition, a broad Raman mode emerging below $\sim 175$ K exhibits an order-parameter-like temperature evolution and coincides with the onset of phonon anomalies, while no corresponding strong anomaly is observed in the two-magnon response. Taken together, these results establish TbFeO$_3$ as a system with pronounced interplay among lattice dynamics, spin correlations, and emergent local magnetic-lattice anomalies.

</details>


### [109] [Spin-orbit-entangled frustrated magnetism in fcc Ba$_2$(Yb,Nd)NbO$_6$ double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01022)
*S. M. Hossain,Sk. Soyeb Ali,S. Mohanty,R. Kolay,M. P. Saravanan,A. K. Yogi,Y. Tokiwa,R. Nath,S. K. Panda,M. Majumder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀土基双钙钛矿Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆，发现其具有面心立方结构中由强自旋轨道耦合和晶体场效应产生的jeff=1/2 Kramers双重态基态，并在毫开温度下表现出长程磁有序，揭示了其作为稀土自旋-轨道纠缠磁体的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev候选材料的探索长期集中在4d和5d过渡金属化合物，而稀土4f体系的研究多局限于蜂窝和三角晶格。本文旨在拓展稀土体系的面心立方晶格方向，寻找新型Kitaev相互作用候选材料。

Method: 采用磁化率测量、比热测量（含毫开尔文温区热力学测量）表征磁性质，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算支持电子结构分析，确定基态和磁有序行为。

Result: 两种化合物均具有由强SOC和晶体场效应产生的孤立jeff=1/2 Kramers双重态基态；毫开温度测量揭示两者均存在中等阻挫的长程磁有序，该磁有序可由有序-无序（order-by-disorder）机制理解。

Conclusion: Ba₂YbNbO₆和Ba₂NdNbO₆被确认为有前景的稀土自旋-轨道纠缠磁体，需进一步实验和理论工作以确定完整交换张量，阐明微观磁相互作用的起源。

Abstract: The search for candidate Kitaev materials has largely focused on 4$d$ and 5$d$ transition-metal compounds with various lattice geometries. In contrast, investigations of rare-earth 4$f$ systems have thus far been restricted mainly to honeycomb and triangular lattices. In this work, we investigate the rare-earth-based double perovskites Ba$_2$YbNbO$_6$ and Ba$_2$NdNbO$_6$, which crystallize in a face-centered cubic structure. Magnetization and heat-capacity measurements establish isolated ${j_{\rm eff}} = 1/2$ Kramers doublet ground states arising from strong spin-orbit coupling (SOC) and crystal electric-field effects, which are further supported by density-functional theory calculations. Millikelvin-temperature thermodynamic measurements reveal long-range magnetic order with moderate frustration in both compounds. The emergence of magnetic order may be understood within an order-by-disorder scenario, as theoretically proposed for rare-earth fcc lattices with finite Kitaev interactions. Our results thus identify Ba$_2$YbNbO$_6$ and Ba$_2$NdNbO$_6$ as promising rare-earth spin-orbit-entangled magnets and motivate further experimental and theoretical investigations aimed at determining the complete exchange tensor to elucidate the microscopic origin of the underlying magnetic interactions.

</details>


### [110] [Magnetic circular dichroism of THz modes and selection rules of Raman-active optical phonons in the polar altermagnet candidate \ce{Mn2Mo3O8}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01062)
*F. Schilberth,K. Vasin,M. Knauft,M. Kondákor,M. Vuckovic,N. Herrmann,K. Penc,M. Minola,B. Keimer,V. A. Martinez,K. Panda,A. A. Sirenko,L. Prodan,V. Tsurkan,A. A. Tsirlin,I. Kézsmárki,J. Deisenhofer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用拉曼散射和太赫兹时域光谱研究了交替磁体候选材料Mn₂Mo₃O₈的磁性和振动激发，识别了所有光学声子模式（包括此前未被观测到的低频模式），并发现磁有序态下太赫兹波段出现强磁圆二色性，但未观测到预期的声子劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究交替磁体（altermagnet）候选材料Mn₂Mo₃O₈中磁有序对晶格振动和低能磁激发的影响，特别是探索磁有序态下光学声子的圆偏振劈裂现象以及太赫兹波段的磁光响应。

Method: 采用变温拉曼散射和磁光太赫兹时域透射光谱进行实验测量，并结合第一性原理计算（ab initio calculations）精确获取振动本征模式的频率，通过赝角动量守恒分析拉曼选择定则。

Result: （1）识别了Mn₂Mo₃O₈中所有光学声子，包括此前研究中遗漏的最低频A₁和E₂型拉曼模式；（2）分析了顺磁相和磁有序相的声子选择定则，但未观测到预期的E₂简并声子在磁有序下的圆偏振劈裂，归因于Mn²⁺的弱自旋-轨道耦合；（3）在磁有序态的太赫兹波段观测到强磁圆二色性，该宽激发带可能源于双磁振子激发，仅具有电偶极活性，且呈现场依赖的双组分精细结构，其磁圆二色性在4 T的自旋翻转（spin-flop）转变以上消失。

Conclusion: Mn₂Mo₃O₈中声子对磁有序的响应受限于弱自旋-轨道耦合，但太赫兹波段的双磁振子激发展现出显著的磁圆二色性，并对外磁场敏感，为交替磁体的低能磁激发研究提供了新的光谱学视角。

Abstract: We investigated the magnetic and vibrational excitations in the collinear altermagnet candidate \ce{Mn2Mo3O8} by temperature dependent Raman scattering and magneto-optical THz time-domain transmission spectroscopy. By comparison to \textit{ab initio} calculations accurately capturing the eigenfrequencies of the vibrational eigenmodes, we identify all optical phonons, including the lowest-lying Raman modes of $A_1$ and $E_2$ type, which had remained elusive in a previous Raman study. Moreover, we compare the selection rules for optically active phonons in the paramagnetic and the magnetically ordered phases of \ce{Mn2Mo3O8} and analyze the Raman selection rules with respect to pseudo-angular momentum conservation. No evidence of the expected splitting of the degenerate paramagnetic $E_2$ optical phonons into modes with circular polarization upon magnetic ordering could be resolved, likely due to weak spin-orbit coupling typical for Mn$^{2+}$. In contrast, we observe strong magnetic circular dichroism at a broad THz excitation band, emerging in the magnetically ordered state. This band, potentially originating from two-magnon excitations, is only electric-dipole active and features a field-dependent two-component fine structure. Its magnetic circular dichroism vanishes above the spin-flop transition at 4~T.

</details>


### [111] [Pressure-induced self-doping and Fermi surface reconstruction in UAs2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01244)
*Zhenchao Wu,Yingying Cao,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过DFT+DMFT计算，揭示了UAs2在压力下轨道选择性的电荷重分布（5f5/2→5f7/2）驱动了从近藤晶格态到混合价态的低能电子结构重构，超导电性出现在费米面具有两片分立且嵌套增强的压力区间，但在费米面弯曲合并成三维柱面时消失，提示费米面嵌套和电荷涨落可能对超导Tc有贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明重费米子化合物UAs2在压力下出现超导电性（具有铀基相关5f电子超导体中最高的Tc）的微观起源。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT），在常压和加压条件下研究UAs2的电子结构。

Result: 常压下成功复现了Kondo晶格电子结构，平带杂化带位于Γ和M点附近，与ARPES实验吻合。压力下发现电子从局域化的5f5/2轨道向更巡游的5f7/2轨道转移（总5f占据数几乎不变），形成一种自掺杂效应，使5f5/2电子从局域Kondo区进入混合价区。超导电性仅在费米面呈两片分立且具有增强嵌套的区间出现，费米面弯曲合并后超导消失。

Conclusion: 提供了理解UAs2超导电性的电子结构基础，指出费米面嵌套和电荷涨落可能对增强超导Tc有贡献，暗示其可能区别于常规重费米子超导体的机制。

Abstract: Superconductivity has recently been reported in the heavy-fermion compound UAs2 under pressure, with the highest Tc among uranium-based correlated 5f-electron superconductors. To elucidate its microscopic origin, we investigate its electronic structure using density functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT). At ambient pressure, our calculations reproduce the characteristic Kondo-lattice electronic structure, with flat hybridization bands near the Fermi energy around the Γ and M points, in good agreement with angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). Under pressure, we find a systematic transfer of electrons from the more localized 5f5/2 orbitals to the more itinerant 5f7/2 orbitals, while the total U-5f occupancy remains nearly unchanged. This orbital-selective charge redistribution constitutes a pressure-induced self-doping effect that drives the 5f5/2 electrons from a localized Kondo regime toward a mixed-valence regime with enhanced charge fluctuations, leading to a dramatic reconstruction of the low-energy electronic structure. Remarkably, superconductivity emerges in the pressure range where the Fermi surface consists of two disconnected sheets with enhanced nesting, but disappears when they bend and merge into a corrugated three-dimensional cylinder. Our results provide an electronic-structure basis for understanding superconductivity in UAs2 and suggest that Fermi-surface nesting and charge fluctuations may contribute to the enhanced superconducting Tc, pointing to a possible distinction from conventional heavy-fermion superconductors.

</details>


### [112] [All-Electric Topological Phase Transitions in Proximity-Coupled Bilayer MnBi2Te4 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01312)
*Basavaraja G,Mukul Kabir*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需外部磁场的全电控方案，通过MnBi2Te4与铁磁绝缘体界面的磁近邻效应，实现轴子绝缘态与量子反常霍尔态之间的可逆拓扑相变，同时显著提升磁有序温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在磁性拓扑绝缘体MnBi2Te4在二维极限下虽可呈现轴子态和量子反常霍尔态，但两者间的拓扑相变需要超过9 T的极端外磁场，严重限制了实际可调性和器件应用。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算与有效哈密顿模型，利用铁磁绝缘体界面的磁近邻效应实现无场调控；通过栅压调节铁磁绝缘体的磁各向异性，动态调制近邻交换偏置，实现对层间反铁磁耦合和能带拓扑的全电学切换；并采用长程海森堡蒙特卡洛模拟评估磁有序温度。

Result: 栅压可调的磁近邻效应实现了无需外磁场的拓扑相变可逆切换；同时，封装后的MnBi2Te4薄膜磁有序温度大幅提升，热稳定性显著改善。

Conclusion: 该栅压调控范式消除了高场需求并提升了热稳定性，解决了拓扑物理中的关键瓶颈，为可扩展的高温拓扑电子学提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The intrinsic magnetic topological insulator MnBi2Te4, in the two-dimensional limit, hosts thickness dependent axion and quantum anomalous Hal (QAH) insulating states governed by antiferromagnetic interlayer coupling. However, controlled interconversion between these phases typically requires extreme external magnetic fields exceeding 9 T, limiting practical tunability. Using complementary first-principles calculations and effective Hamiltonian modeling, we demonstrate a field-free, reversible mechanism to engineer topological phase transitions by exploiting magnetic proximity at the interfaces with a ferromagnetic insulator. Gate-tunable magnetic anisotropy within the ferromagnetic insulator dynamically modulates the proximity-induced exchange bias, enabling all-electric switching of interlayer coupling and band topology in ultrathin MnBi2Te4. Crucially, long-range Heisenberg Monte Carlo simulations reveal that the magnetic ordering temperature of the encapculated MnBi2Te4 film is dramatically elevated. By eliminating the high-field requirement and simultaneously improving thermal stability, this gate-tunable paradigm solves a critical bottleneck in topological physics and offers a viable route toward scalable, high-temperature topological electronics.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum geometry and RKKY in flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01363)
*Chang-geun Oh,Makoto Shimizu,Youichi Yanase,Shuichi Murakami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在平带极限下，RKKY相互作用并非由群速度熄灭而消失，而是由布洛赫态的量子几何（特别是量子度量）所介导；量子度量决定了磁关联长度、自旋刚度和有序温度，并可绕过Mermin-Wagner定理的热力学极限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的RKKY相互作用图像依赖于传导电子的色散和有限群速度，但在平带系统中群速度趋于零，这一传统机制面临根本性挑战。作者试图阐明平带极限下局域磁矩间有效耦合的替代机制。

Method: 从微观RKKY推导出发，分析静态磁化率的布里渊区平均量子度量对其长波结构的控制作用，并建立量子度量与磁关联长度、自旋刚度及有序温度的理论关联。

Result: 布里渊区平均的量子度量决定了静态磁化率的长波行为；瓦尼尔函数的有限空间展宽在无单粒子色散时提供了有效的长程耦合通道；量子度量增强了磁刚性，并在有限尺寸系统中提升临界温度，从而绕过了Mermin-Wagner定理的限制。

Conclusion: 平带系统中的RKKY相互作用由量子几何而非群速度主导，量子度量成为调控磁性有序的关键物理量，为有限和低维系统中的磁性研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Flat conduction bands quench the group velocity and thus challenge conventional, dispersion-driven pictures of the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interaction, where localized moments are coupled via an effective exchange mediated by conduction electrons. Here we show that RKKY interactions in the flat-band limit are not extinguished by the vanishing group velocity but are instead mediated by the quantum geometry of Bloch states. Starting from a microscopic RKKY derivation, we demonstrate that the Brillouin-zone-averaged quantum metric controls the long-wavelength structure of the static susceptibility, thereby determining the magnetic correlation length and the spin stiffness. As a result, the finite spatial spread of Wannier functions provides an effective long-range coupling channel even when single-particle dispersion is absent. Furthermore, we establish the general principle that the ordering temperature is governed by the quantum metric in finite and low-dimensional samples, effectively circumventing the thermodynamic-limit constraint of the Mermin-Wagner theorem. Specifically, our theoretical investigation reveals that increasing the quantum metric enhances magnetic rigidity and leads to a corresponding rise in the critical temperature within finite-sized systems.

</details>


### [114] [Emergent modular Luttinger liquid from spin-partitioned entanglement in the one-dimensional Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01817)
*Ádám Bácsi,Catalin Pascu Moca,Balázs Dóra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究一维排斥Hubbard模型的旋量分区纠缠哈密顿量，结合玻色化和精确对角化，发现迹掉一种自旋物种后产生一个模Luttinger液体，其纠缠谱无色散、具有动量无关的纠缠能隙，但本征态代数关联由电荷和自旋Luttinger参数的几何平均决定，纠缠谱呈现普适的分支层次结构，模基态与无自旋Luttinger液体几乎相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一维相互作用费米子系统中，通过对自旋自由度进行分区取迹后，纠缠哈密顿量所揭示的普适模结构，旨在理解纠缠谱与物理系统本身谱性质之间的根本差异。

Method: 结合玻色化（bosonization）解析方法和精确对角化（exact diagonalization）数值方法，研究自旋分区（spin-partitioned）纠缠哈密顿量。通过迹掉一种自旋物种，构造模Luttinger液体理论框架，分析其纠缠谱、本征态关联函数及模基态性质。

Result: 1. 模谱完全无色散，具有与动量无关的纠缠能隙；2. 本征态的代数关联由单一有效Luttinger参数控制，该参数等于电荷和自旋Luttinger参数的几何平均；3. 纠缠谱呈现普适的分支层次结构，与精确对角化结果高度一致；4. 模基态几乎等同于无自旋Luttinger液体的基态。

Conclusion: 在一维相互作用费米子系统中，通过自旋分区取迹揭示了普适的模结构：纠缠哈密顿量描述的是一个模Luttinger液体，其性质由电荷和自旋自由度的几何平均有效参数统一刻画，且纠缠谱的普适分支层次结构为相互作用一维量子多体系统的纠缠特征提供了深刻洞见。

Abstract: We study the spin-partitioned entanglement Hamiltonian of the one-dimensional repulsive Hubbard model. By combining bosonization with exact diagonalization, we show that tracing out one spin species produces a modular Luttinger liquid, whose properties fundamentally differ from those of the physical system. While the modular spectrum is fully dispersionless and possesses a momentum-independent entanglement gap, its eigenstates exhibit algebraic correlations governed by a single effective Luttinger parameter equal to the geometric mean of the charge and spin Luttinger parameters. The resulting entanglement spectrum displays a universal branching hierarchy in excellent agreement with exact diagonalization. We further demonstrate that the modular ground state is nearly identical to that of the spinless Luttinger liquid. These results uncover a universal modular structure in interacting one-dimensional fermionic systems.

</details>


### [115] [Topological Defects in Triple-$Q$ Magnetic Orders: A Fixed-Lattice Homotopy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01838)
*Jin-Tao Jin,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对M点三重-Q Ginzburg-Landau理论中N=2和3的所有七个稳定相进行了体缺陷分类，揭示了连续自旋旋转与离散晶体对称性耦合产生的丰富缺陷结构，包括手性畴壁、Z₂框架涡旋以及分数缠绕与离散畴壁的线性禁闭等新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重-Q磁序将连续自旋旋转与平移和点群变换相关的离散晶体扇区相结合，产生比传统单-Q磁体更丰富的缺陷结构。作者旨在系统分类M点三重-Q Ginzburg-Landau理论框架下所有稳定相的体缺陷，填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: 采用对称性分析方法，基于(V₄⋊D₃)×O(N)对称性的Ginzburg-Landau理论（其中V₄为平移生成的Klein四元群），将原子晶格视为预设背景，排除晶格位错和向错，保持三个Fourier场在物理M点的标签。通过计算参考态稳定子群H及连通分量G₀/(H∩G₀)，区分投影商与真实连通流形，系统推导各相的缺陷分类。

Result: 关键结果包括：(1) 正交三重-Q相的完整流形为O(3)，具有手性畴壁和阿贝尔Z₂框架涡旋，而非非阿贝尔二元多面体涡旋；(2) 每个O(2)相的连通分量支持整数2π涡旋，分数缠绕仅在附着于离散畴壁时闭合，且在非零壁张力下呈线性禁闭；(3) 平移对称性禁止交叉梯度双线性项，将二次弹性扇区简化为各向同性和M点锁定的各向异性刚度。

Conclusion: 该分类将三重-Q磁体中的自由内部缺陷、晶体畴壁和壁束缚复合体明确分离，为理解多重-Q磁序的拓扑缺陷物理提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Multiple-$Q$ magnetic orders combine continuous spin rotations with discrete crystalline sectors associated with translations and point-group transformations, producing a richer defect structure than conventional single-$Q$ magnets. We classify the bulk defects of all seven stable phases for $N=2$ and $3$ in the $M$-point triple-$Q$ Ginzburg--Landau theory with $(\Vfour\rtimes\Dthree)\times\OO(N)$ symmetry, where $\Vfour$ is the translation-generated Klein four-group. The atomic lattice is treated as a prescribed background, with lattice dislocations and disclinations excluded and the three Fourier fields retaining their physical $M$-point labels. The parent-group transformations continuously connected to the identity form $G_0=\{e\}\times\SO(N)$. For a reference-state stabilizer $H$, the connected component containing the reference state is $G_0/(H\cap G_0)$, not the quotient obtained by projecting $H$ onto spin space. This distinction gives the orthogonal triple-$Q$ phase the full manifold $\OO(3)$, with chirality walls and Abelian $\ZZ_2$ frame vortices rather than non-Abelian binary-polyhedral vortices. Every connected component of the $\OO(2)$ phases supports an integer $2π$ vortex, whereas fractional windings close only when attached to a discrete-domain wall and are linearly confined at nonzero wall tension. Translation symmetry further forbids cross-gradient bilinears, reducing the quadratic elastic sector to an isotropic and an $M$-point-locked anisotropic stiffness. The classification separates free internal defects, crystalline domain walls, and wall-bound composites in triple-$Q$ magnets.

</details>


### [116] [Diodes and capacitors for the transport of monopoles in fragmented spin ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01883)
*Anoop Raj,Sumiran Pujari,Ludovic D. C. Jaubert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了在自旋冰材料中利用磁畴壁设计磁单极子二极管和电容器的理论方案，通过磁碎片化体系中长程反铁磁序与自旋冰物理的共存，实现了对磁单极子输运的整流和电荷分离功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋冰中磁单极子动力学已被深入研究，但如何设计器件来控制磁流（magnetricity）几乎空白。本研究旨在填补这一空白，为磁单极子的定向输运和存储提供可行的器件设计方案。

Method: 利用磁碎片化（magnetic fragmentation）体系中自旋冰物理与长程反铁磁序共存的特性，通过特定条件制备畴壁，使其成为磁单极子的不对称滤波器——正电荷可通过而负电荷被排斥（反向则相反），从而实现二极管功能。相邻畴壁可分离正负电荷并形成电荷真空区，构成电容器。通过超过百万自旋的模拟验证该理论方案。

Result: 成功证明畴壁可作为磁单极子二极管，实现定向整流；相邻畴壁可构成磁单极子电容器，充电后理论上可作为磁单极子电池使用。所有微观机制均得到解释，并通过大规模自旋模拟验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁碎片化自旋冰中利用畴壁控制磁单极子输运提供了理论证明，并讨论了在稀土烧绿石氧化物和人工自旋冰中的实验应用前景。进一步指出，畴壁可被视为由时间反演对称性分隔的两个镜像"世界"之间的涌现边界，并将研究方向拓展至手性和向列型自旋液体中涌现准粒子穿越畴壁的动力学。

Abstract: Spin-ice materials are famous for their quasi-particle excitations that behave like magnetic monopoles. Magnetricity is the concept that these monopoles can conduct an AC magnetic current, in analogy with conduction electrons. While monopole dynamics has been intensively studied and is reasonably well understood, very little has been done to design devices in order to control magnetricity. Here we develop a theoretical proof of concept for the design of diodes and capacitors for the transport of monopoles. We use the property of systems with magnetic fragmentation, where spin-ice physics co-exists with long-range antiferromagnetic order. The key point is that magnetic order allows for the existence of domain walls. Under certain conditions of preparation, this domain wall is equivalent to an asymmetric filter for monopoles. In a given direction, positive charges can go through while negative ones are repelled; the opposite applies in the opposite direction. This asymmetry effectively functions like a diode for monopole current. Successive domain walls separate positive from negative charges with a vacuum of charge in between, producing a capacitor for monopoles. Once the capacitor is charged, it can in principle be used as a battery for monopoles. All microscopic mechanisms are explained and our proof of concept is validated by simulations of more than a million spins. Application to experiments are discussed for rare-earth pyrochlore oxides and artificial spin ice. Finally, we discuss in general terms how a domain wall in fragmented spin ice can also be seen as an emergent boundary separating two mirror "worlds" separated by time-reversal symmetry. Beyond spin ice, our work opens a promising direction of investigation for the dynamics of emergent quasi-particles crossing domain walls in chiral and nematic spin liquids, which also possess a broken symmetry.

</details>


### [117] [Microscopic magnetic phase evolution in the Weyl semimetal Mn$_3$Sn revealed by $μ^+$SR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01892)
*U. Miniotaite,O. K. Forslund,H. Luetkens,V. Rai,J. Persson,G. D. Morris,D. Zuhair,D. W. Tam,S. Nandi,Y. Sassa,M. Månsson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过μ子自旋弛豫和体磁化测量，研究了反铁磁Weyl半金属Mn₃Sn的复杂磁相图，发现低温区无静态自旋玻璃态，而是铁磁成分增强与局域自旋涨落减慢并存；非公度螺旋相中存在非对称内场分布，且在升温过程中与公度相反三角相广泛共存；高温区存在μ子位点占据数的温度驱动变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mn₃Sn虽已知存在公度反三角AFM相、非公度螺旋AFM相及可能的低温自旋玻璃态，但其低温态的真实性质、各磁相之间的演化关系及共存行为尚不清晰。该工作旨在利用μSR对局域磁环境的高灵敏度，澄清这些争议并揭示其动态磁景观。

Method: 采用μ子自旋弛豫（μ⁺SR）技术（包括零场ZF谱和横场TF谱）结合体磁化测量，对名义成分为Mn₂.₉₉Sn的单晶样品在宽温区（覆盖T_f=21 K到T_N=418 K）进行系统研究，通过谱线拟合提取内场分布、弛豫率、初始不对称度等参数。

Result: （1）低温T_f=21 K以下无静态自旋玻璃证据，而是出现增强的铁磁成分和局域化自旋涨落减慢，二者可能解耦；（2）非公度螺旋相中ZF谱拟合揭示非对称内场分布，表明磁结构被非谐性显著修饰；（3）150 K以上升温时μ子谱权重连续再分布，显示非公度螺旋相与公度反三角相广泛共存；（4）公度反三角相中存在约20%的μ子缺失分数，归因于未分辨的超快退极化；（5）325 K以上观察到μ子位点占据数的温度驱动变化。

Conclusion: Mn₃Sn展现出高度动态的磁景观，其复杂的磁有序相往往随温度连续演化并广泛共存，而非以尖锐相变边界截然分离。低温态并非传统自旋玻璃，其铁磁性与自旋动力学可能具有不同起源。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive muon spin relaxation ($μ^+$SR) and bulk magnetization study of the antiferromagnetic (AFM) Weyl semimetal Mn$_3$Sn (composition Mn$_{2.99}$Sn). Mn$_3$Sn is reported to exhibit a commensurate inverse triangular (IT) AFM phase, an incommensurate (IC) helical AFM phase, and a proposed low-temperature spin-glass-like state. In our sample, we establish the respective transition temperatures for these phases to be $T_\mathrm{N} = 418$ K, $T_\mathrm{t} \approx 275$ K, and $T_\mathrm{f} = 21$ K. Investigating the low-temperature regime below $T_\mathrm{f}$, we find no evidence of a static spin-glass state. Instead, the sample exhibits an increasing ferromagnetic (FM) component accompanied by a localized slowing of spin fluctuations, indicating that these phenomena may be decoupled. In the IC helical AFM phase, fitting the zero-field (ZF) spectra reveals an asymmetric internal magnetic-field distribution, indicating that the magnetic structure is heavily modified by anharmonicity. Upon warming above 150 K, a continuous redistribution of muon spectral weight reveals a broad regime of coexisting local magnetic environments associated with the IC helical and IT-AFM phases. In the commensurate IT-AFM phase above $T_\mathrm{t}$, a persistent missing fraction in the initial asymmetry indicates that a subset of implanted muons, corresponding to roughly 20% of the sample-related asymmetry, undergoes unresolved ultrafast depolarization. Finally, we observe temperature-driven shifts in muon site populations above 325 K. Ultimately, our results show a highly dynamic magnetic landscape in Mn$_3$Sn, demonstrating how its complex magnetic orders often coexist and evolve continuously with temperature.

</details>


### [118] [Pressure-Driven Evolution of Electronic and Magnetic Correlations in Bilayer Nickelate La3Ni2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02261)
*Jian Zhou,Rui Song,Haiyan Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 高压下双层La3Ni2O7中，压力增强层间跃迁的同时使系统趋于更巡游状态，导致dx2-y2轨道巡游性增强而dz2轨道保持局域化。这种轨道选择性演化强化了近藤屏蔽效应，抑制了作为配对胶水的有效磁交换耦合Jeff，从而解释了高Tc超导在高压区单调下降及穹顶形相图的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在加压双层La3Ni2O7中发现的高温超导性引发了广泛关注，但其压力依赖的超导转变温度Tc的微观机制仍不明确。特别是Tc随压力呈穹顶形变化（先升后降）的物理根源亟待解释。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)、约束随机相近似(cRPA)和动力学平均场理论(DMFT)相结合的多方法联用方案，系统研究高压下La3Ni2O7的电子关联和磁关联演化。

Result: (1) 静水压同时增强层间跃迁和裸超交换能标(4t²/U)，但也通过降低相对关联强度(U/W)驱动系统走向巡游态；(2) 发现轨道选择性演化：Ni dx2-y2态越来越巡游，dz2轨道保持局域化；(3) 压力诱导的巡游性显著增强两轨道间的杂化，导致巡游dx2-y2电子对局域dz2磁矩的近藤屏蔽急剧放大；(4) 有效磁交换耦合Jeff因此在高压区被抑制。

Conclusion: 高压下Tc的单调下降由近藤屏蔽对超交换作用的压倒性优势所驱动，为La3Ni2O7的穹顶形超导相图提供了自洽的微观解释。

Abstract: The recent discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in pressurized bilayer La3Ni2O7 has sparked intense research interest, yet the microscopic mechanism governing its pressure-dependent superconducting transition temperature (Tc) remains elusive. In this work, we investigate the electronic and magnetic correlations of La3Ni2O7 under high pressure using a combination of density-functional theory (DFT), constrained random phase approximation (cRPA), and dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). We find that while hydrostatic pressure enhances the interlayer hopping and the bare superexchange energy scale (4t2/U), it simultaneously drives the system toward a more itinerant regime by reducing the relative correlation strength (U/W). Crucially, our results reveal a distinct orbital-selective evolution: the Ni dx2-y2 states become increasingly itinerant, whereas the Ni dz2 orbitals retain a more localized character. This pressure-induced itinerancy significantly enhances the hybridization between the two, leading to a dramatic amplification of the Kondo-like screening of the local dz2 moments by the itinerant dx2-y2 electrons. Consequently, the effective magnetic exchange coupling (Jeff), which serves as the pairing glue, is suppressed in the high-pressure regime. Our findings suggest that the monotonic decrease of Tc at high pressures is driven by the dominance of Kondo screening over superexchange interactions, providing a coherent microscopic explanation for the dome-shaped superconducting phase diagram in La3Ni2O7.

</details>


### [119] [Long and short time linear response of metals: a geometric approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02298)
*Nishchhal Verma,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了量子几何在金属低能物理中的作用，发现费米面附近波函数的量子几何可产生显著效应。作者计算了金属的含时量子几何张量，解释了其发散行为，并与狄拉克/外尔半金属的奇异性进行对比，提出以Drude权重与总谱权重之比作为区分束缚电荷与巡游电荷的晶格尺度探针，并在kagome金属中进行了量化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何张量通常被认为在金属的低能描述中处于次要地位，因为金属的物理由带内过程主导。本文旨在挑战这一传统观点，揭示费米面附近波函数的量子几何在特定场景下可扮演重要角色。

Method: 作者计算了金属中的含时量子几何张量，分析其发散特性，并与狄拉克半金属和外尔半金属的奇异几何张量进行对比。进一步，将Drude谱权重与总谱权重之比 D/S₁ 定义为区分束缚电荷与巡游电荷的晶格尺度探针，并在kagome金属的两个van Hove填充情形下进行量化分析。

Result: （1）金属的含时量子几何张量存在发散行为；（2）该发散与狄拉克/外尔半金属中几何张量的奇异性具有不同特征；（3）D/S₁ 比值能有效在晶格尺度上区分束缚电荷与巡游电荷；（4）在kagome金属中，两个van Hove填充尽管具有相同的费米面，但几何响应截然不同。

Conclusion: 量子几何在金属低能物理中并非总是可以忽略，费米面附近的波函数几何能显著影响电子性质。D/S₁ 作为实验可观测量，为探测量子几何效应提供了实用工具，kagome金属中的差异响应进一步凸显了几何效应的丰富性。

Abstract: The time-dependent quantum geometric tensor, which captures dipole fluctuations of bound electrons, is essential for understanding the electronic properties of insulators, superconductors, and flat bands. It is often considered subleading for low-energy descriptions of metals that are dominated by intra-band processes. Here, we revisit this perspective and highlight scenarios where the quantum geometry of the wavefunctions close to the Fermi surface plays a significant role. We compute the time-dependent quantum geometric tensor for metals, explain its divergence, and contrast it against singular geometric tensors of Dirac and Weyl semi-metals. We identify the ratio of Drude to total spectral weight, $D/\mathcal{S}_1$, as a lattice-scale probe of bound versus itinerant charge, and quantify it in the kagome metal, where the two van Hove fillings respond differently despite identical Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [120] [Optimized Tensor-Network Renormalization for Quantum Dynamics: Resolving the Spectral Function of $\mathrm{K_2Co(SeO_3)_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02473)
*Jiahang Hu,Runze Chi,B. Normand,Hai-Jun Liao,T. Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 针对无限投影纠缠对态（iPEPS）框架下动力学谱函数计算中的数值不稳定性，作者提出了激发态裁剪的角转移矩阵重整化群（ET-CTMRG）方法，通过将激发张量纳入重整化流程，显著降低了截断误差，并在三角晶格XXZ磁体的超固相中与中子散射实验取得了定量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于iPEPS的动力学谱函数方法仅从基态环境构建重整化张量，存在严重的数值不稳定性，限制了该方法在二维量子系统动力学研究中的广泛应用。

Method: 提出ET-CTMRG方法：将激发张量引入角转移矩阵重整化群（CTMRG）的重整化流程中，使有效哈密顿量矩阵的构建更为精确，从而获得稳定、收敛的激发谱。

Result: 在Heisenberg反铁磁体中，截断误差降低了数个量级；在三角晶格XXZ磁体K₂Co(SeO₃)₂的超固相中，计算结果与非弹性中子散射实验实现了出色的定量吻合。

Conclusion: ET-CTMRG为强关联量子系统的动力学性质研究提供了一个稳健且可靠的张量网络框架。

Abstract: Tensor-network methods have opened a powerful route for the study of dynamical spectral functions in two-dimensional quantum systems. However, existing approaches within the framework of infinite projected entangled-pair states construct the required renormalization tensors solely from the ground-state environment and can suffer from severe numerical instability. We identify the origin of this instability and introduce an excitation-tailored corner-transfer-matrix renormalization-group (ET-CTMRG) method to resolve it. By incorporating excitation tensors into the renormalization procedure, the method constructs a substantially more accurate effective Hamiltonian matrix and thereby yields reliable and well-converged excitation spectra. For Heisenberg antiferromagnets, it reduces truncation errors by orders of magnitude and for the particularly complex case of the supersolid phase in the triangular-lattice XXZ magnet $\mathrm{K_2Co(SeO_3)_2}$, it achieves excellent quantitative agreement with inelastic neutron-scattering measurements. ET-CTMRG therefore provides a robust framework for investigating the dynamical properties of strongly correlated quantum systems.

</details>

### [121] [An asymptotically solvable model of many-body critical phases: mobility edges, scars, and inverted scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00157)
*Yi-Ting Tu,Zi-Jian Li,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个一维相互作用自旋链的可解模型，其空间结构诱导了镜像多体共振层级。作者发现在热力学极限下存在两个相，分别满足和违反弱本征态热化假说（ETH），且两相之间可由有限温度相变（即热力学多体迁移率边）分隔。此外，在无低无序区域的准周期MBL系统中也可能存在离域的稀有区域，挑战了准周期MBL中无雪崩失稳的普遍看法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机MBL系统的预热化区域由偶然多体共振主导，但在大尺寸确定性系统中，源自底层势结构的多体共振可能产生不同的物理。这类共振可导致单粒子临界相，但其在相互作用系统中的后果尚不清楚。作者旨在探究确定性结构中由势结构诱导的多体共振对相互作用系统的影响。

Method: 作者构造了一个一维最近邻相互作用自旋链的渐近可解模型，利用其空间结构诱导镜像多体共振层级，并通过弱ETH的满足与否来表征热力学极限下的两个相，同时分析稀有本征态（多体疤痕与逆疤痕）的行为。

Result: (1) 推导出两个相：满足弱ETH的相（类似ETH相）和违反弱ETH的相（类似MBL相），但存在稀有本征态表现为相反相行为（多体疤痕与逆疤痕）。(2) 两相可由有限温度相变分隔，即存在热力学多体迁移率边，这与以往认为不可能的共识相悖。(3) 结果表明，即使在无低无序区域的准周期MBL系统中，也可能存在离域的稀有区域，挑战了准周期MBL无雪崩失稳的传统观点。

Conclusion: 确定性系统中的势结构诱导多体共振可产生与随机MBL系统不同的物理现象，包括有限温度多体迁移率边和离域稀有区域。这为理解准周期MBL系统的稳定性提供了新视角，并暗示雪崩失稳可能在准周期系统中同样存在。

Abstract: While the prethermal regime of random many-body localized (MBL) systems is dominated by accidental many-body resonances, another class of resonances, originating from the underlying potential structure, is expected in large-size deterministic systems. It is known that this class of resonances can lead to single-particle critical phases that are neither localized nor extended, but the consequences in interacting systems remain unclear. In this work, we construct an asymptotically solvable model of a one-dimensional nearest-neighbor interacting spin chain, whose spatial structure induces a hierarchy of mirror-like many-body resonances. We derive two phases in the thermodynamic limit, characterized by the satisfaction and violation of a version of the weak eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). While these two phases are similar to the usual MBL and ETH phases, there exist rare eigenstates that behave like the opposite phase, interpreted as many-body scars and inverted scars. Surprisingly, the two phases can be separated by a finite-temperature phase transition, corresponding to a thermodynamic many-body mobility edge, which was often believed to be impossible. Our results also suggest the existence of delocalized rare regions in an otherwise-localized interacting Aubry-André model, even if there are no low-disorder regions like those in random systems. This challenges the common belief that there is no avalanche instability in quasiperiodic MBL.

</details>


### [122] [Neural Quantum States for Nuclear Magnetic Resonance Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00178)
*Bharadwaj Chowdary Mummaneni,Bo Xing*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文将神经量子态（NQS）作为变分量子态表示，用于从第一性原理预测核磁共振（NMR）光谱，通过TDVP和p-tVMC两种传播方法在2至5自旋实验分子上进行了基准测试，并提出在化学位移哈密顿量的相互作用框架中传播以大幅减少积分步数，从而将方法扩展至14自旋的蔗糖分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一性原理预测NMR光谱需要对$2^N$维量子态进行传播，对于较大$N$个耦合自旋系统在计算上不可行，因此需要寻找新的替代表示方法来解决这一指数级复杂度问题。

Method: 采用神经量子态（NQS）作为变分波函数表示，结合两种含时传播方法：时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）和投影时间依赖变分蒙特卡罗（p-tVMC）。进一步提出在化学位移哈密顿量的相互作用框架中传播的策略，以降低光谱带宽对积分步数的依赖。

Result: TDVP在所有谱线位置和强度上均方误差小于$10^{-3}$；p-tVMC精度取决于每步优化参数。相互作用框架传播使3自旋系统积分步数减少约8倍，4和5自旋系统减少至少一个数量级，并成功实现对14自旋蔗糖分子的精确蒙特卡罗采样传播。

Conclusion: NQS结合相互作用框架传播是一种有前景的方法，可从第一性原理高效预测中等规模分子体系的NMR光谱，显著突破传统方法在较大自旋系统上的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Predicting a nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectrum from first principles requires propagating a quantum state of dimension $2^N$ for $N$ coupled spins, which becomes intractable beyond larger $N$. We benchmark Neural Quantum States (NQS), a class of variational quantum states expressed as an artificial neural network, as an alternative representation for this problem. Using two propagation methods, the Time-Dependent Variational Principle (TDVP) and projected time-dependent Variational Monte Carlo (p-tVMC), we compute the $^1$H spectra of four ($2 \to 5$ spins) experimentally parameterized molecules. TDVP reproduces all line positions and intensities with average spectral mean squared errors of $<10^{-3}$; p-tVMC reproduces the same features, with accuracy determined by its per-step optimization parameters. One dominant obstacle to larger systems is the steep growth of the number of integration steps with spectral bandwidth, which can be removed by propagating in the interaction frame of the chemical-shifted Hamiltonian. Retaining the same accuracy, this reduces the number of integration steps roughly eightfold for the 3-spin system and by at least an order of magnitude for the 4- and 5-spin systems, and it enables a 14-spin molecule (sucrose) to be accurately propagated via Monte Carlo sampling.

</details>


### [123] [Spin Quantum Hall Effect: the Critical Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00849)
*Hrant Topchyan,Win Nuding,Andreas Klümper,Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文对自旋量子霍尔效应网络模型进行了高精度数值计算，利用S矩阵方法确定了局域化长度指数ν和边界临界指数μ，为无序拓扑系统和共形场论提供了重要的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋量子霍尔效应是少数已知精确临界指数的安德森局域化转变实例，是检验无序拓扑系统和共形场论理论的重要平台。但对应的网络模型（属于Altland-Zirnbauer分类的C类）的临界指数仍需高精度数值确定。

Method: 采用最近发展的S矩阵方法对随机网络进行高精度数值计算，将Chalker-Coddington模型中的随机U(1)相位替换为随机SU(2)矩阵，构建属于对称类C的SQHE网络模型。

Result: 通过高精度数值计算，获得了自旋量子霍尔效应网络模型的局域化长度指数ν和边界临界指数μ的数值结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为SQHE网络模型提供了高精度的临界指数数值结果，这对验证无序拓扑系统理论和共形场论预测具有重要意义，同时支持该模型描述破缺时间反演对称性的二维无序超导体中准粒子输运的观点。

Abstract: The spin quantum Hall effect (SQHE) provides one of the few examples of an Anderson localization transition for which exact critical exponents are known, making it an important testing ground for theories of disordered topological systems and conformal field theory. The corresponding network model, obtained by replacing the random $U(1)$ phases of the Chalker--Coddington model with random $SU(2)$ matrices, belongs to symmetry class C of the Altland--Zirnbauer classification and is believed to describe quasiparticle transport in two-dimensional disordered superconductors with broken time-reversal symmetry. In this work, we perform high-precision numerical calculations of the localization-length exponent ($ν$) and the boundary critical exponent ($μ$) for the SQHE network model using the recently developed $S$-matrix approach to random networks.

</details>


### [124] [Predictive Formulas for Scattering Mean Free Path for General Disordered Dielectric Media Beyond the Long-Wavelength Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01016)
*Jaeuk Kim,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 推导了适用于任意形状多分散颗粒介质和非颗粒介质的散射平均自由程预测公式，将微观结构通过谱密度引入，并揭示了超均匀介质中散射平均自由程的标度律及隐身超均匀介质的透明窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mie理论的散射平均自由程估计仅限于相同圆形或球形散射体，无法处理任意形状、多分散颗粒介质及非颗粒介质。需要一种基于微观结构的通用预测方法。

Method: 基于有效动态介电常数的精确强对比展开(exact strong-contrast expansion)推导预测公式，将微观结构通过谱密度(spectral density)引入；采用有限差分时域(FDTD)模拟验证五种非超均匀和超均匀模型；对比Mie理论适用范围。

Result: 当k₁/s ≲ 1时，公式预测与FDTD模拟吻合良好，且在二维TM极化下精度优于Mie理论；k₁/s ≳ 1时Mie估计更准确。超均匀介质中，若̃χ_V(k)~k^α，则ℓ_s ~ k₁^{-(d+1+α)}；隐身超均匀介质在有限波数区间内完全透明。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条基于微观结构预测和设计无序介电材料中波传输的通用途径，突破了传统Mie理论的局限性。

Abstract: We derive predictive formulas for the scattering mean free path $\ell_s$ of statistically homogeneous two-phase dielectric media in dimensions $d=1,2,3$. Unlike Mie-based estimates limited to identical circular or spherical scatterers, the formulas apply to arbitrarily shaped and polydisperse particulate media as well as nonparticulate media, with microstructure entering through the spectral density. The formulas are based on the exact strong-contrast expansion for the effective dynamic dielectric constant. We apply them to five nonhyperuniform and hyperuniform models and validate selected cases using finite-difference time-domain simulations. For $k_1/s \lesssim 1$, where $k_1$ is the incident wavenumber and $s$ is the specific surface, the predictions agree well with simulations and are consistent with Mie theory where applicable, while improving accuracy for two-dimensional transverse-magnetic polarization. Mie estimates become more accurate for $k_1/s \gtrsim 1$. For hyperuniform media with $\widetildeχ_V(k)\sim k^α$ at small $k$, the theory predicts $\ell_s\sim k_1^{-(d+1+α)}$; stealthy hyperuniform media are transparent over a finite wavenumber interval. These results provide a microstructure-based route to predict and design wave transport in general disordered dielectric materials.

</details>


### [125] [Griffiths phase in clique percolation in random geometric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01487)
*Vasilii Tiselko,Olga Valba,Alexander Gorsky*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究了不同几何约束核下随机几何图系综中的团簇渗流，发现尖锐截断核存在具有幂律行为的宽广Griffiths相，幂律核展现出团簇尺寸依赖的ER-几何区域边界，指数核的模式也被分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索几何约束对随机图中团簇渗流行为的影响，理解不同核函数（尖锐截断、幂律、指数）如何调控渗流相变的临界行为，特别是Griffiths相的存在性和特征。

Method: 在随机几何图系综中引入不同几何核函数（尖锐截断核、幂律核、指数核）来量化几何约束，分析团簇渗流（clique percolation）的相变行为，识别相边界和临界区域的标度特性。

Result: 尖锐截断核下发现宽广的Griffiths相，呈现幂律行为，该相的两个边界分别对应大有限团簇内渗流涌现点和整体团簇系统渗流可用点；幂律核展现出更丰富的行为，存在团簇尺寸依赖的有效ER区域与几何区域边界，同样存在Griffiths相；指数核的模式也得到分析。

Conclusion: 几何约束核的形式对随机图中的团簇渗流行为有显著调控作用，Griffiths相在不同核函数下普遍存在，这些发现可能在网络科学、复杂系统等领域具有应用价值。

Abstract: In this study, we discuss the clique percolation in the ensembles of random geometric graphs with different kernels that quantify the geometrical constraints. For the sharp cut-off we find the wide Griffiths phase of extended criticality with the power-law behavior. One boundary of the Griffiths phase is the generalization of a percolation critical point for the ER ensemble when the percolation within the large but finite cluster emerges. The second boundary corresponds to the point in the parameter space when the percolation in the entire clustered system becomes available. For the power-law kernel, richer behavior with a clique-size-dependent boundary between the effective ER and geometric regimes has been identified. The Griffiths phase in this case exists as well. Finally, the pattern with the exponential kernel has been analyzed. We briefly discuss the possible applications of our findings.

</details>


### [126] [Tunneling the Loss Landscape: Bypassing Memorization with Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01833)
*Lai Shun Chan,Xiaotian Zhang,Yue Shang,Ge Zhang,Entao Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过参数移动性（PM）、副本相关性（RC）和分形维度（FD）三个指标，实证验证了grokking现象与玻璃化动力学的对应关系，并提出受swap Monte Carlo启发的SAM-Swap优化插件以加速泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将grokking类比为计算玻璃弛豫过程，但该视角主要停留在宏观理论层面，缺乏对训练动力学的直接实证验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，直接刻画训练过程中的玻璃动力学特征。

Method: 提出三组分框架：参数移动性（PM）衡量参数空间中的运动能力，副本相关性（RC）反映历史依赖性，分形维度（FD）刻画运动轨迹的几何特征。基于玻璃动力学的定量分析，提出State-Aware Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping (SAM-Swap)，受swap Monte Carlo算法启发，作为加速泛化的优化插件。

Result: 标准优化展现出明确的玻璃动力学特征：grokking网络被捕获在动力学停滞的记忆状态中，表现为移动性崩溃、强历史依赖和通道状运动。SAM-Swap能有效加速泛化，且加速泛化与参数空间中的随机探索（类似物理中的扩散）一致相关。

Conclusion: grokking可被定量理解为一种玻璃化动力学现象，通过引入类似swap Monte Carlo的参数交换机制可以有效打破动力学停滞，加速从记忆到泛化的相变过程。

Abstract: Grokking is a striking phenomenon in neural network training, where a model can undergo a prolonged period of pure memorization before abrupt generalization. While previous works have attempted to interpret it through classical machine learning mechanisms like weight norm, recent research draws an analogy from statistical physics, framing grokking as a form of computational glass relaxation. This theory defines the initial memorization as a result of `fast cooling' where the training loss is reduced so quickly that a glass state is formed, followed by a `slow relaxation' towards final generalization. Although providing a unifying framework for representative grokking theories, this perspective has remained largely at the theoretical on macroscopic level without direct empirical validation on training dynamics. Here we introduce a three-component framework to directly characterize the training dynamics via parameter mobility (PM), and two representative measurements from glassy dynamics: replica correlation (RC) and fractal dimension (FD). We demonstrate that standard optimization presents clear signatures of glass dynamics and inherently traps the grokking network in a kinetic arrested memorization state with a collapsed mobility, strong history dependence, and channel-like motions. This quantitative agreement motivates us to introduce State-Aware Monte Carlo Parameter Swapping (SAM-Swap), an optimization plug-in that can accelerate generalization, inspired by swap Monte Carlo algorithm widely used in glass dynamics. Comparing SAM-Swap, weight decay, and Gaussian gradient noise, we find that accelerated generalization is consistently associated with random exploration in the parameter space, similar to diffusion in physics.

</details>


### [127] [Bayesian Inference with Structured Signal: Static Replica Symmetry Breaking on the Nishimori Line in the Planted Spin Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02373)
*Andrea Vincenzo Dell'Abate,Louise Budzynski*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了相关信号先验对贝叶斯推断的影响，通过随机正则图上的 planted 自旋玻璃模型，发现先验的相关结构在顺磁区有助于推断（降低重构阈值），在铁磁区先验本身即可部分恢复，而在副本对称破缺（RSB）区则会在后验中诱导出静态 RSB 转变，为贝叶斯最优推断中出现静态 RSB 提供了一个罕见实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯推断理论模型通常假设信号分量独立同分布（i.i.d.），但现实中信号往往存在相关性。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，系统研究信号先验中的相关结构如何影响推断的可行性与算法性能。

Method: 采用 planted 自旋玻璃在随机正则图上的最小模型，信号从耦合系数为 κ 的伊辛模型中采样。通过改变先验的相态（顺磁、铁磁、RSB），分析后验分布的性质，并在 Nishimori 条件下检测静态 RSB 转变，同时讨论信念传播（BP）算法的表现。

Result: （1）顺磁区：信号相关性降低重构阈值，更弱的信号强度即可实现恢复；（2）铁磁区：先验本身已能部分恢复，识别了观测提供额外信息的阈值；（3）RSB 区：在 Nishimori 条件下检测到后验的静态 RSB 转变，这为非分离、相关先验导致贝叶斯最优推断中出现静态 RSB 提供了实例。

Conclusion: 信号先验的相关结构对推断有显著且相态依赖的影响：既可能帮助也可能阻碍推断。更重要的是，相关先验可在贝叶斯最优设定中引发静态 RSB，这为理解相关信号推断的算法复杂性（尤其是信念传播的失效）提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: A common assumption in theoretical models of Bayesian inference is that the signal has i.i.d. components. To study the effect of correlations in the signal prior, we consider a minimal model: the planted spin glass on random regular graphs, where the signal is sampled from an Ising model with coupling $κ$. Depending on the phase of the prior, we find that adding structure in the signal can either help or hinder inference. In the paramagnetic regime, correlations in the signal lower the reconstruction threshold, so that weaker signal strength is sufficient for recovery. In the ferromagnetic regime, the prior alone already enables partial recovery, and we identify the threshold above which the observations provide additional information. When the prior itself is in a replica symmetry breaking (RSB) phase, we detect a static RSB transition in the posterior under Nishimori conditions. This provides an example where a non-separable, correlated prior leads to static RSB in a Bayes-optimal inference problem. We discuss the consequences of this glassy phase for algorithmic performance, in particular for Belief Propagation.

</details>
